+// Above half the smaller side the bezel already covers the whole glass, so a larger
+// edge_thickness_px could only flatten the centre's falloff further.
+inline constexpr float EDGE_THICKNESS_PX_MAX_FRACTION = 0.5f;
+
+// Bezel width as a fraction of the glass shape's smaller side. edge_thickness_px is in
+// logical pixels (scaled like bevel_size); when it is > 0 it replaces the relative
+// edge_thickness, which is returned untouched otherwise.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr float effectiveEdgeThickness(float relative, float pixelsLogical, float monitorScale,
+                                                     float minDimensionPhysical) noexcept {
+    if (!(pixelsLogical > 0.0f) || !(minDimensionPhysical > 0.0f))
+        return relative;
+    const float scale = monitorScale > 0.0f ? monitorScale : 1.0f;
+    return std::min(pixelsLogical * scale / minDimensionPhysical, EDGE_THICKNESS_PX_MAX_FRACTION);
+}
+
+static_assert(effectiveEdgeThickness(0.06f, 0.0f, 1.0f, 400.0f) == 0.06f);
+static_assert(effectiveEdgeThickness(0.06f, 12.0f, 1.0f, 400.0f) == 0.03f);
+static_assert(effectiveEdgeThickness(0.06f, 12.0f, 2.0f, 800.0f) == 0.03f);
+static_assert(effectiveEdgeThickness(0.06f, 10000.0f, 1.0f, 400.0f) == EDGE_THICKNESS_PX_MAX_FRACTION);
+static_assert(effectiveEdgeThickness(0.06f, -5.0f, 1.0f, 400.0f) == 0.06f);
+static_assert(effectiveEdgeThickness(0.06f, 12.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f) == 0.06f);
+static_assert(effectiveEdgeThickness(0.06f, 12.0f, 1.0f, -10.0f) == 0.06f);
+
+// edge_height, logical px. Far past any useful wall: it only keeps the shader's
+// products of height and slope finite.
+inline constexpr float EDGE_HEIGHT_MAX = 500.0f;
+
+// How far outside the glass the lens rim's reflection reads, framebuffer px. Inside
+// SAMPLE_PADDING_PX, so it reads sampled backdrop, never the clamped texture border.
+inline constexpr float LENS_REFLECTION_REACH_PX = 0.8f * SAMPLE_PADDING_PX;
+static_assert(LENS_REFLECTION_REACH_PX > 0.0f && LENS_REFLECTION_REACH_PX < SAMPLE_PADDING_PX);
+
+// Must match the `regionRects[64]` array size declared in Shaders.hpp. 64 lets a client trace a
+// rounded shape to within a pixel or two; at 16 a capsule's curve steps by several pixels, and
+// anything the client draws just outside the shape (a glow, a shadow) falls inside the steps.

diff --git a/src/GlassSnapshotElement.cpp b/src/GlassSnapshotElement.cpp
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a0edcd7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/GlassSnapshotElement.cpp
@@ -0,0 +1,172 @@
+#include "GlassSnapshotElement.hpp"
+#include "Diagnostics.hpp"
+#include "GlassRenderer.hpp"
+#include "Globals.hpp"
+#include "RenderGuards.hpp"
+
+#include <optional>
+#include <GLES3/gl32.h>
+#include <hyprland/src/render/gl/GLFramebuffer.hpp>
+#include <hyprland/src/render/OpenGL.hpp>
+
+// About 10 s at 60 Hz of this monitor's own frames without a request.
+static constexpr uint64_t IDLE_FRAMES = 600;
+
+// The blit needs raw GL framebuffer ids. A framebuffer that is not GL-backed
+// skips the snapshot rather than being dereferenced through a failed cast.
+static std::optional<GLuint> glFramebufferId(const SP<Render::IFramebuffer>& framebuffer) {
+    auto* glFramebuffer = dynamic_cast<Render::GL::CGLFramebuffer*>(framebuffer.get());
+    if (!glFramebuffer)
+        return std::nullopt;
+    return glFramebuffer->getFBID();
+}
+
+static bool matchesFrame(const SP<Render::IFramebuffer>& snapshot, const SP<Render::IFramebuffer>& frame) {
+    return snapshot && frame && snapshot->m_size == frame->m_size && snapshot->m_drmFormat == frame->m_drmFormat;
+}
+
+// The render damage is already in framebuffer pixels (the pass is drawn unrotated);
+// clipped to the monitor like CRenderPass::simplify() clips element damage, since
+// the frame damage can reach past it.
+static CRegion framebufferDamage(const PHLMONITOR& monitor, const CRegion& damage) {
+    CRegion region = damage.copy();
+    region.intersect(CBox{{}, monitor->m_transformedSize});
+    return region;
+}
+
+bool xrayHasBackground() {
+    const auto& config = g_pGlobalState->config;
+    return !readBoolConfig(config.xpMode);
+}
+
+void requestXraySnapshot(PHLMONITOR monitor) {
+    if (!g_pGlobalState || !monitor)
+        return;
+
+    auto& snapshot            = g_pGlobalState->backgroundSnapshots[monitor->m_id];
+    snapshot.lastRequestFrame = snapshot.monitorFrames;
+}
+
+void beginXraySnapshotFrame(Render::CRenderContext& ctx) {
+    const auto monitor = ctx.m_data.pMonitor.lock();
+    if (!g_pGlobalState || !monitor)
+        return;
+
+    auto&      snapshots = g_pGlobalState->backgroundSnapshots;
+    const auto it        = snapshots.find(monitor->m_id);
+    if (it == snapshots.end())
+        return;
+    auto& snapshot = it->second;
+
+    // Here rather than at PRE_WINDOWS, which a solitary fullscreen client skips:
+    // its frames still age the snapshot out.
+    if (++snapshot.monitorFrames > snapshot.lastRequestFrame + IDLE_FRAMES) {
+        snapshots.erase(it);
+        Diagnostics::recordXrayEvict(monitor->m_id);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Fires for every frame, solitary and mirror frames included, so a frame
+    // that never reaches PRE_WINDOWS still leaves its damage invalid.
+    if (!snapshot.valid.empty())
+        snapshot.valid.subtract(framebufferDamage(monitor, ctx.m_data.damage));
+}
+
+void queueXraySnapshot(Render::CRenderContext& ctx) {
+    // Not the monitor's own frame: an overview plugin emits this stage while
+    // rendering its own framebuffer, which has the windows in it already.
+    if (!g_pGlobalState || RenderGuards::isForeignRender(ctx) || ctx.m_renderingSnapshot || ctx.m_data.projectionType != Render::RPT_MONITOR)
+        return;
+
+    const auto monitor = ctx.m_data.pMonitor.lock();
+    const auto source  = ctx.m_data.currentFB;
+    if (!monitor || !source || source->m_size.x <= 0 || source->m_size.y <= 0)
+        return;
+
+    const auto it = g_pGlobalState->backgroundSnapshots.find(monitor->m_id);
+    if (it == g_pGlobalState->backgroundSnapshots.end())
+        return;
+    auto& snapshot = it->second;
+
+    // A later PRE_WINDOWS of the same frame (special workspace) has windows under it.
+    if (snapshot.queuedFrame == snapshot.monitorFrames)
+        return;
+    snapshot.queuedFrame = snapshot.monitorFrames;
+
+    // Format as well as size: an FP16 frame blitted into an 8-bit snapshot would
+    // band the glass and convert on every copy.
+    if (!matchesFrame(snapshot.framebuffer, source)) {
+        snapshot.valid.clear();
+        if (!snapshot.framebuffer)
+            snapshot.framebuffer = g_pHyprRenderer->createFB("hyprglass-xray");
+        if (!snapshot.framebuffer->alloc(static_cast<int>(source->m_size.x), static_cast<int>(source->m_size.y), source->m_drmFormat))
+            return;
+    }
+
+    g_pHyprRenderer->currentPass(ctx).add(makeUnique<CGlassSnapshotElement>());
+}
+
+SP<Render::IFramebuffer> xraySnapshotCovering(PHLMONITOR monitor, const SP<Render::IFramebuffer>& frame, const CBox& box) {
+    if (!g_pGlobalState || !monitor)
+        return nullptr;
+
+    const auto it = g_pGlobalState->backgroundSnapshots.find(monitor->m_id);
+    if (it == g_pGlobalState->backgroundSnapshots.end())
+        return nullptr;
+
+    const auto& snapshot = it->second;
+    if (!matchesFrame(snapshot.framebuffer, frame) || !GlassRenderer::sampleRegionCovered(box, snapshot.framebuffer, snapshot.valid))
+        return nullptr;
+
+    return snapshot.framebuffer;
+}
+
+std::vector<UP<IPassElement>> CGlassSnapshotElement::draw(Render::CRenderContext& ctx) {
+    if (!g_pGlobalState)
+        return {};
+
+    const auto monitor = ctx.m_data.pMonitor.lock();
+    const auto source  = ctx.m_data.currentFB;
+    if (!monitor || !source)
+        return {};
+
+    const auto it = g_pGlobalState->backgroundSnapshots.find(monitor->m_id);
+    if (it == g_pGlobalState->backgroundSnapshots.end())
+        return {};
+    auto& snapshot = it->second;
+
+    if (!matchesFrame(snapshot.framebuffer, source))
+        return {};
+
+    const auto sourceId   = glFramebufferId(source);
+    const auto snapshotId = glFramebufferId(snapshot.framebuffer);
+    if (!sourceId || !snapshotId)
+        return {};
+
+    // Only this element's damage holds freshly drawn background: the work buffer
+    // is cleared elsewhere, and occluded damage was never drawn.
+    CRegion copied = framebufferDamage(monitor, ctx.m_data.damage);
+    copied.intersect(CBox{{}, source->m_size});
+    if (copied.empty())
+        return {};
+
+    // The pass scissors each element to its damage, which would clip the blit.
+    g_pHyprOpenGL->setCapStatus(GL_SCISSOR_TEST, false);
+    // the tracker skips glDisable when it believes the test is already off
+    if (glIsEnabled(GL_SCISSOR_TEST)) {
+        glDisable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
+        Diagnostics::recordStateDesync("scissor on before the x-ray copy");
+    }
+
+    g_pHyprOpenGL->bindFramebuffer(GL_READ_FRAMEBUFFER, *sourceId);
+    g_pHyprOpenGL->bindFramebuffer(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER, *snapshotId);
+    for (const auto& rect : copied.getRects())
+        glBlitFramebuffer(rect.x1, rect.y1, rect.x2, rect.y2, rect.x1, rect.y1, rect.x2, rect.y2, GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT, GL_NEAREST);
+
+    snapshot.valid.add(copied);
+    Diagnostics::recordXrayCopy(monitor->m_id);
+
+    // Leave the renderer's target bound the way it expects it.
+    source->bind();
+    return {};
+}

+#pragma once
+
+#include <hyprland/src/helpers/memory/Memory.hpp>
+#include <hyprland/src/render/pass/PassElement.hpp>
+#include <hyprland/src/render/Renderer.hpp>
+
+// X-ray: copies the frame's damaged region into the monitor's snapshot
+// framebuffer. Queued at RENDER_PRE_WINDOWS, so the snapshot holds the desktop
+// with no window in it.
+class CGlassSnapshotElement : public IPassElement {
+  public:
+    CGlassSnapshotElement()           = default;
+    ~CGlassSnapshotElement() override = default;
+
+    std::vector<UP<IPassElement>> draw(Render::CRenderContext& ctx) override;
+    [[nodiscard]] bool            needsLiveBlur(Render::CRenderContext&) override { return false; }
+    [[nodiscard]] bool            needsPrecomputeBlur(Render::CRenderContext&) override { return false; }
+
+    [[nodiscard]] const char*      passName() override { return "CGlassSnapshotElement"; }
+    [[nodiscard]] ePassElementType type() override { return EK_CUSTOM; }
+};
+
+// False under render:xp_mode, which draws no wallpaper or bottom layers to show.
+[[nodiscard]] bool xrayHasBackground();
+
+// Marks the monitor's snapshot as still wanted this frame, creating it on the
+// first ask. Called while the pass is built, never from inside it.
+void requestXraySnapshot(PHLMONITOR monitor);
+
+// RENDER_BEGIN: advances the rendered monitor's frame count, drops a snapshot
+// nothing asked for lately and cuts this frame's damage from its valid region.
+void beginXraySnapshotFrame(Render::CRenderContext& ctx);
+
+// RENDER_PRE_WINDOWS: sizes the snapshot to the frame and queues the copy.
+void queueXraySnapshot(Render::CRenderContext& ctx);
+
+// The monitor's snapshot when it matches `frame` and holds every pixel
+// sampleBackground() reads for `box` (framebuffer pixels), else nullptr.
+[[nodiscard]] SP<Render::IFramebuffer> xraySnapshotCovering(PHLMONITOR monitor, const SP<Render::IFramebuffer>& frame, const CBox& box);

diff --git a/src/HyprlandIdentity.hpp b/src/HyprlandIdentity.hpp
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..73a76b5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/HyprlandIdentity.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,121 @@
+#pragma once
+
+#include <array>
+#include <charconv>
+#include <optional>
+#include <string>
+#include <string_view>
+#include <format>
+#include <utility>
+
+// Whether the Hyprland headers hyprglass was built against describe the
+// running Hyprland. No Hyprland include, so it builds and tests standalone.
+namespace HyprlandIdentity {
+
+struct SReleaseLine {
+    int major = 0;
+    int minor = 0;
+
+    bool operator==(const SReleaseLine&) const = default;
+};
+
+// "v0.56.2" -> 0.56. git describe output ("v0.56.0-175-g4bb6844"), "" and
+// "unknown" are not releases.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::optional<SReleaseLine> releaseLine(std::string_view tag) noexcept {
+    if (!tag.starts_with('v'))
+        return std::nullopt;
+    tag.remove_prefix(1);
+    int parts[3] = {};
+    for (int index = 0; index < 3; ++index) {
+        const auto [end, error] = std::from_chars(tag.data(), tag.data() + tag.size(), parts[index]);
+        if (error != std::errc{} || end == tag.data())
+            return std::nullopt;
+        tag.remove_prefix(static_cast<size_t>(end - tag.data()));
+        if (index < 2) {
+            if (!tag.starts_with('.'))
+                return std::nullopt;
+            tag.remove_prefix(1);
+        }
+    }
+    if (!tag.empty())
+        return std::nullopt;
+    return SReleaseLine{parts[0], parts[1]};
+}
+
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool isCommitHash(std::string_view hash) noexcept {
+    if (hash.size() != 40)
+        return false;
+    for (const char character : hash)
+        if (!((character >= '0' && character <= '9') || (character >= 'a' && character <= 'f')))
+            return false;
+    return true;
+}
+
+enum class EVerdict {
+    Match,
+    Mismatch,
+    Unknown, // no usable tag or commit on one side
+};
+
+// Same commit, else same release line when both are releases, else
+// different commits. Nix builds of hyprland-git report "v" + VERSION as
+// their tag, so they look like releases here: commitOnly skips the release
+// line for a build whose Hyprland API changes between commits, and a running
+// Hyprland without a commit is then never the one built against.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr EVerdict compare(std::string_view builtTag, std::string_view builtHash, std::string_view runningTag, std::string_view runningHash,
+                                         bool commitOnly = false) noexcept {
+    if (isCommitHash(builtHash) && builtHash == runningHash)
+        return EVerdict::Match;
+    const auto builtLine   = releaseLine(builtTag);
+    const auto runningLine = releaseLine(runningTag);
+    if (!commitOnly && builtLine && runningLine)
+        return *builtLine == *runningLine ? EVerdict::Match : EVerdict::Mismatch;
+    if (isCommitHash(builtHash) && (commitOnly || isCommitHash(runningHash)))
+        return EVerdict::Mismatch;
+    return EVerdict::Unknown;
+}
+
+// "0.56" for a release, else the commit's first 12 characters.
+[[nodiscard]] inline std::string describe(std::string_view tag, std::string_view hash, bool commitOnly = false) {
+    if (commitOnly && isCommitHash(hash))
+        return std::string(hash.substr(0, 12));
+    if (const auto line = releaseLine(tag))
+        return std::to_string(line->major) + "." + std::to_string(line->minor);
+    return hash.empty() ? std::string("unknown") : std::string(hash.substr(0, 12));
+}
+
+// "<commit>_aq_0.15_hu_0.14_hg_0.5_hc_0.1_hlg_0.6" -> "_aq_0.15_…": the
+// library versions, without the commit.
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::string_view dependencySuffix(std::string_view apiHash) noexcept {
+    const auto at = apiHash.find("_aq_");
+    return at == std::string_view::npos ? apiHash : apiHash.substr(at);
+}
+
+// Keys carry both underscores, so "_hg_" never matches inside "_hlg_".
+[[nodiscard]] constexpr std::string_view dependencyVersion(std::string_view suffix, std::string_view key) noexcept {
+    const auto at = suffix.find(key);
+    if (at == std::string_view::npos)
+        return {};
+    const auto value = suffix.substr(at + key.size());
+    return value.substr(0, value.find('_'));
+}
+
+// "aquamarine 0.15 -> 0.16, hyprlang 0.6 -> 0.7"
+[[nodiscard]] inline std::string dependencyDifferences(std::string_view builtSuffix, std::string_view runningSuffix) {
+    constexpr std::array<std::pair<std::string_view, std::string_view>, 5> LIBRARIES = {{
+        {"_aq_", "aquamarine"}, {"_hu_", "hyprutils"}, {"_hg_", "hyprgraphics"}, {"_hc_", "hyprcursor"}, {"_hlg_", "hyprlang"},
+    }};
+    std::string out;
+    for (const auto& [key, name] : LIBRARIES) {
+        const auto built   = dependencyVersion(builtSuffix, key);
+        const auto running = dependencyVersion(runningSuffix, key);
+        if (built == running)
+            continue;
+        if (!out.empty())
+            out += ", ";
+        out += std::format("{} {} -> {}", name, built, running);
+    }
+    return out;
+}
+
+} // namespace HyprlandIdentity

+++ b/src/LoadGuard.cpp
@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
+#include "LoadGuard.hpp"
+#include "BuildChannel.hpp"
+#include "Globals.hpp"
+
+#include <hyprland/src/plugins/PluginSystem.hpp>
+
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <dlfcn.h>
+#include <format>
+
+namespace LoadGuard {
+
+SVerdict checkCompatibility(HANDLE handle) {
+    const SVersionInfo running = HyprlandAPI::getHyprlandVersion(handle);
+
+    SVerdict verdict;
+    verdict.identity = HyprlandIdentity::compare(GIT_TAG, GIT_COMMIT_HASH, running.tag, running.hash, BUILT_FOR_HYPRLAND_GIT);
+    if (verdict.identity == HyprlandIdentity::EVerdict::Mismatch) {
+        verdict.reason  = EPauseReason::HyprlandVersion;
+        verdict.message = std::format("built for Hyprland {}, running {}", HyprlandIdentity::describe(GIT_TAG, GIT_COMMIT_HASH, BUILT_FOR_HYPRLAND_GIT),
+                                      HyprlandIdentity::describe(running.tag, running.hash, BUILT_FOR_HYPRLAND_GIT));
+        return verdict;
+    }
+
+    // _get_hash() is Hyprland's, _get_client_hash() the one compiled into this plugin.
+    const auto runningLibraries = HyprlandIdentity::dependencySuffix(__hyprland_api_get_hash());
+    const auto builtLibraries   = HyprlandIdentity::dependencySuffix(__hyprland_api_get_client_hash());
+    if (runningLibraries != builtLibraries) {
+        verdict.reason        = EPauseReason::Dependencies;
+        const auto difference = HyprlandIdentity::dependencyDifferences(builtLibraries, runningLibraries);
+        verdict.message       = std::format("built with {}", difference.empty() ? std::string("other Hyprland libraries") : difference);
+    }
+    return verdict;
+}
+
+std::optional<std::string> otherActiveCopyPath() {
+    for (const auto* plugin : g_pPluginSystem->getAllPlugins()) {
+        // The copy being initialised has no name yet; a same-handle entry is
+        // this file loaded through another path (symlink, hardlink).
+        if (plugin->m_name != PLUGIN_NAME)
+            continue;
+        // A paused copy must not keep a working one from loading. Copies older
+        // than this check have no such symbol and are always active.
+        using FInstanceActive     = bool (*)();
+        const auto instanceActive = reinterpret_cast<FInstanceActive>(dlsym(plugin->m_handle, "hyprglass_instance_active"));
+        if (!instanceActive || instanceActive())
+            return plugin->m_path;
+    }
+    return std::nullopt;
+}
+
+bool skipRequested() {
+    const char* skip = std::getenv("HYPRGLASS_SKIP_VERSION_CHECK");
+    return skip && *skip && std::string_view{skip} != "0";
+}
+
+std::string_view reasonCode(EPauseReason reason) noexcept {
+    switch (reason) {
+        case EPauseReason::HyprlandVersion: return "hyprland_version";
+        case EPauseReason::Dependencies: return "dependencies";
+        case EPauseReason::Duplicate: return "duplicate";
+        case EPauseReason::None: break;
+    }
+    return "";
+}
+
+std::string_view checkResult(const SVerdict& verdict) noexcept {
+    if (verdict.reason != EPauseReason::None)
+        return "skipped";
+    return verdict.identity == HyprlandIdentity::EVerdict::Match ? "match" : "unknown";
+}
+
+} // namespace LoadGuard
diff --git a/src/LoadGuard.hpp b/src/LoadGuard.hpp
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2e6a6a5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/LoadGuard.hpp
@@ -0,0 +1,46 @@
+#pragma once
+
+#include "HyprlandIdentity.hpp"
+
+#include <hyprland/src/plugins/PluginAPI.hpp>
+
+#include <optional>
+#include <string>
+#include <string_view>
+
+// Decides, before PLUGIN_INIT touches anything else, whether this copy may run.
+// A paused copy registers nothing: no hook, decoration, listener, config value,
+// hyprctl command, protocol global or GL object.
+namespace LoadGuard {
+
+enum class EPauseReason {
+    None,
+    HyprlandVersion, // built for another Hyprland release line or commit
+    Dependencies,    // same Hyprland, other aquamarine/hyprutils/... versions
+    Duplicate,       // another hyprglass copy is already active
+};
+
+struct SVerdict {
+    EPauseReason               reason   = EPauseReason::None;
+    HyprlandIdentity::EVerdict identity = HyprlandIdentity::EVerdict::Unknown;
+    std::string                message; // "built for Hyprland 0.56, running 0.57"
+};
+
+// Calls only getHyprlandVersion() and __hyprland_api_get_hash(): both
+// extern "C" and unchanged across Hyprland versions, so they are safe to call
+// from a build made for another Hyprland.
+[[nodiscard]] SVerdict checkCompatibility(HANDLE handle);
+
+// Reads Hyprland's plugin list, whose layout comes from our headers: call it
+// only once checkCompatibility() passed. Empty when no other copy is active.
+[[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::string> otherActiveCopyPath();
+
+// HYPRGLASS_SKIP_VERSION_CHECK, read from Hyprland's own environment.
+[[nodiscard]] bool skipRequested();
+
+[[nodiscard]] std::string_view reasonCode(EPauseReason reason) noexcept;
+
+// `versionCheck` in `hyprctl hyprglass status`, for a copy that went on to load.
+[[nodiscard]] std::string_view checkResult(const SVerdict& verdict) noexcept;
+
+} // namespace LoadGuard

+// Far beyond any monitor, but small enough that scaled sums and squares in the
+// shader and the box maths stay finite.
+constexpr double SHAPE_LIMIT_PX = 16384.0;
+
+static float clampShapeInset(double value) {
+    return static_cast<float>(std::clamp(value, -SHAPE_LIMIT_PX, SHAPE_LIMIT_PX));

+// Any negative radius means a capsule.
+static float clampShapeRadius(double value) {
+    return value < 0.0 ? -1.0f : static_cast<float>(std::min(value, SHAPE_LIMIT_PX));
+}

-static void readPresetValuesFromTable(lua_State* L, int tableIdx, SPresetValues& values) {
+static std::optional<double> luaFiniteNumber(lua_State* L, int index) {
+    if (lua_isnumber(L, index)) {
+        const double value = lua_tonumber(L, index);
+        if (std::isfinite(value))
+            return value;
+    }
+    return std::nullopt;
+}
+
+// Booleans read as 0/1, so a flag like xray takes `true` as hyprglass.config() does.
+static std::optional<double> luaPresetValue(lua_State* L, int index) {
+    if (lua_isboolean(L, index))
+        return lua_toboolean(L, index) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
+    return luaFiniteNumber(L, index);
+}
+
+// Stack: the key (a string) at -2 and its value at -1. scope names the table in the messages.
+static void readPresetField(lua_State* L, SPresetValues& values, const std::string& scope) {
+    const std::string_view key = lua_tostring(L, -2);
+
+    float*   floatField = presetFloatField(values, key);
+    int64_t* intField   = floatField ? nullptr : presetIntField(values, key);
+    if (!floatField && !intField) {
+        reportLuaError(L, "preset", std::format("unknown setting '{}' in '{}'", key, scope));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const auto value = luaPresetValue(L, -1);
+    if (!value) {
+        reportLuaError(L, "preset", std::format("'{}' in '{}' expects a number or true/false", key, scope));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (floatField)
+        *floatField = static_cast<float>(*value);
+    else
+        *intField = static_cast<int64_t>(std::clamp(*value, -9.0e18, 9.0e18)); // keeps the cast defined
+}
+

+// shape = { inset = px or {top, right, bottom, left}, radius = px }, both optional (0).
+static std::optional<SLayerShape> readLuaLayerShape(lua_State* L, int tableIdx) {
+    SLayerShape shape;
+    bool        valid = true;
+
+    lua_getfield(L, tableIdx, "inset");
+    if (lua_isnumber(L, -1)) {
+        const double inset = lua_tonumber(L, -1);
+        valid = valid && std::isfinite(inset);
+        shape.insetTop = shape.insetRight = shape.insetBottom = shape.insetLeft = clampShapeInset(inset);
+    } else if (lua_istable(L, -1)) {
+        const int                   insetIdx = lua_gettop(L);
+        const std::array<float*, 4> sides    = {&shape.insetTop, &shape.insetRight, &shape.insetBottom, &shape.insetLeft};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < sides.size(); i++) {
+            lua_rawgeti(L, insetIdx, static_cast<lua_Integer>(i + 1));
+            const double side = lua_isnumber(L, -1) ? lua_tonumber(L, -1) : std::nan("");
+            valid             = valid && std::isfinite(side);
+            *sides[i]         = clampShapeInset(side);
+            lua_pop(L, 1);
+        }
+    } else if (!lua_isnil(L, -1)) {
+        valid = false;
+    }
+    lua_pop(L, 1);
+
+    lua_getfield(L, tableIdx, "radius");
+    if (lua_isnumber(L, -1)) {
+        const double radius = lua_tonumber(L, -1);
+        valid               = valid && std::isfinite(radius);
+        shape.radius        = clampShapeRadius(radius);
+    } else if (!lua_isnil(L, -1)) {
+        valid = false;
+    }
+    lua_pop(L, 1);
+
+    if (!valid)
+        return std::nullopt;
+    return shape;
+}

+// A Hyprland key stored as a bool: read through its reported type, so a key that
+// reports anything else reads as unset instead of as a misread byte.
+struct BoolConfigPtr {
+    void* const*          dataptr = nullptr;
+    const std::type_info* type    = nullptr;
+};
+
+inline bool readBoolConfig(const BoolConfigPtr& ptr) {
+    if (!ptr.dataptr || !ptr.type || *ptr.type != typeid(bool))
+        return false;
+
+    return **reinterpret_cast<const bool* const*>(ptr.dataptr);
+}

+bool CShaderManager::compileContourPrograms() {
+    const auto& vertex = g_pHyprOpenGL->m_shaders->TEXVERTSRC;
+
+    // dynamic: a program the driver rejects comes back false instead of aborting the compositor
+    if (!m_contour.seed->createProgram(vertex, loadShaderSource("contourseed.frag"), true, true) ||
+        !m_contour.edt->createProgram(vertex, loadShaderSource("contouredt.frag"), true, true) ||
+        !m_contour.smooth->createProgram(vertex, loadShaderSource("contoursmooth.frag"), true, true) ||
+        !m_contour.pack->createProgram(vertex, loadShaderSource("contourfield.frag"), true, true))
+        return false;
+
+    auto locate = [](const SP<CShader>& shader, const char* name) { return glGetUniformLocation(shader->program(), name); };
+
+    auto& seed         = m_contour.seedUniforms;
+    seed.tex           = locate(m_contour.seed, "tex");
+    seed.boxOffsetPx   = locate(m_contour.seed, "boxOffsetPx");
+    seed.boxSizePx     = locate(m_contour.seed, "boxSizePx");
+    seed.sourceSizePx  = locate(m_contour.seed, "sourceSizePx");
+    seed.divisor       = locate(m_contour.seed, "divisor");
+    seed.threshold     = locate(m_contour.seed, "threshold");
+
+    auto& edt          = m_contour.edtUniforms;
+    edt.tex            = locate(m_contour.edt, "tex");
+    edt.fieldSize      = locate(m_contour.edt, "fieldSize");
+    edt.direction      = locate(m_contour.edt, "direction");
+    edt.reachTexels    = locate(m_contour.edt, "reachTexels");
+    edt.resolveSigned  = locate(m_contour.edt, "resolveSigned");
+    edt.texelPx        = locate(m_contour.edt, "texelPx");
+    edt.reachPx        = locate(m_contour.edt, "reachPx");
+
+    auto& smooth             = m_contour.smoothUniforms;
+    smooth.tex               = locate(m_contour.smooth, "tex");
+    smooth.fieldSize         = locate(m_contour.smooth, "fieldSize");
+    smooth.direction         = locate(m_contour.smooth, "direction");
+    smooth.radius            = locate(m_contour.smooth, "radius");
+    smooth.negHalfInvSigmaSq = locate(m_contour.smooth, "negHalfInvSigmaSq");
+
+    auto& pack     = m_contour.packUniforms;
+    pack.tex       = locate(m_contour.pack, "tex");
+    pack.fieldSize = locate(m_contour.pack, "fieldSize");
+    pack.texelPx   = locate(m_contour.pack, "texelPx");
+
+    return true;
+}
+
+const SContourPrograms* CShaderManager::contourPrograms() {
+    if (m_contourCompiled)
+        return &m_contour;
+    if (m_contourFailed)
+        return nullptr;
+
+    if (compileContourPrograms()) {
+        m_contourCompiled = true;
+        return &m_contour;
+    }
+
+    m_contourFailed = true;
+    m_contour.seed->destroy();
+    m_contour.edt->destroy();
+    m_contour.smooth->destroy();
+    m_contour.pack->destroy();
+    HyprlandAPI::addNotification(PHANDLE,
+        std::format("[{}] The contour shaders failed to compile, layers fall back to mask_mode = alpha", PLUGIN_NAME),
+        CHyprColor{1.0, 0.6, 0.2, 1.0}, 8000);
+    return nullptr;
+}

+    }

 }
 
+SGlassProgram& CShaderManager::glassProgram(GlassFeatureMask features) {
+    features &= GLASS_FEATURE_ALL;
+    if (features == GLASS_FEATURE_NONE)
+        return m_defaultGlass;
+
+    auto [variant, inserted] = m_glassVariants.try_emplace(features);
+    if (inserted)
+        variant->second.features = features;
+    if (inserted && !compileGlassProgram(variant->second, features)) {
+        variant->second.failed = true;
+        HyprlandAPI::addNotification(PHANDLE,
+            std::format("[{}] An optional glass effect failed to compile (features {:#x}), glass is drawn without it",
+                        PLUGIN_NAME, features),
+            CHyprColor{1.0, 0.6, 0.2, 1.0}, 8000);
+    }
+
+    return variant->second.failed ? m_defaultGlass : variant->second;
+}
+

+    m_defaultGlass.shader->destroy();
+    m_defaultGlass.uniforms = {};
+    for (auto& [features, variant] : m_glassVariants)
+        variant.shader->destroy();
+    m_glassVariants.clear();
     blurShader->destroy();
+    if (m_contourCompiled) {
+        m_contour.seed->destroy();
+        m_contour.edt->destroy();
+        m_contour.smooth->destroy();
+        m_contour.pack->destroy();
+    }
+    m_contour         = {};
+    m_contourCompiled = false;
+    m_contourFailed   = false;
     m_initialized   = false;
     m_compileFailed = false;
 }

diff --git a/src/ShaderVariants.hpp b/src/ShaderVariants.hpp
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d7e05ee

@@ -0,0 +1,75 @@
+#pragma once
+
+#include <algorithm>
+#include <array>
+#include <cstdint>
+#include <format>
+#include <string>
+#include <string_view>
+
+// Optional glass effects. Each one is compiled into its own variant of the glass
+// program, behind a GLSL #define, so a draw that leaves every effect off runs the
+// default program, unchanged, and pays nothing for effects it does not use: a
+// runtime branch in one big program costs every draw registers and occupancy.
+using GlassFeatureMask = uint32_t;
+
+inline constexpr GlassFeatureMask GLASS_FEATURE_NONE = 0;
+// edge_height > 0: the rim is a lens that refracts (and can mirror) what is around it.
+inline constexpr GlassFeatureMask GLASS_FEATURE_LENS_EDGE = 1u << 0;
+// focus_light > 0 on the active window: a light along its glass edge.
+inline constexpr GlassFeatureMask GLASS_FEATURE_FOCUS_LIGHT = 1u << 1;
+// frost > 0 and windows behind the glass: the backdrop is blurred further.
+inline constexpr GlassFeatureMask GLASS_FEATURE_FROST = 1u << 2;
+// A layer with mask_mode = contour: its edge follows a traced outline (Contour.hpp).
+inline constexpr GlassFeatureMask GLASS_FEATURE_CONTOUR = 1u << 3;
+
+struct SGlassFeatureDefine {
+    GlassFeatureMask bit;
+    std::string_view name; // defined to 1 in the variant's source
+};
+
+// One entry per feature bit.
+inline constexpr std::array GLASS_FEATURE_DEFINES{
+    SGlassFeatureDefine{GLASS_FEATURE_LENS_EDGE, "HYPRGLASS_FEATURE_LENS_EDGE"},
+    SGlassFeatureDefine{GLASS_FEATURE_FOCUS_LIGHT, "HYPRGLASS_FEATURE_FOCUS_LIGHT"},
+    SGlassFeatureDefine{GLASS_FEATURE_FROST, "HYPRGLASS_FEATURE_FROST"},
+    SGlassFeatureDefine{GLASS_FEATURE_CONTOUR, "HYPRGLASS_FEATURE_CONTOUR"},
+};
+
+[[nodiscard]] consteval GlassFeatureMask allGlassFeatures() {
+    GlassFeatureMask all = GLASS_FEATURE_NONE;
+    for (const auto& feature : GLASS_FEATURE_DEFINES) {
+        if (feature.bit == 0 || (feature.bit & (feature.bit - 1)) != 0 || (all & feature.bit) != 0)
+            throw "each glass feature needs its own single bit";
+        all |= feature.bit;
+    }
+    return all;
+}
+
+inline constexpr GlassFeatureMask GLASS_FEATURE_ALL = allGlassFeatures();
+
+// The glass program's source for `features`. With no feature it is `base` itself,
+// byte for byte; otherwise the features' #defines follow the #version line, then a
+// #line directive keeps compile errors at the default source's line numbers.
+[[nodiscard]] inline std::string glassVariantSource(std::string_view base, GlassFeatureMask features) {
+    std::string source{base};
+    features &= GLASS_FEATURE_ALL;
+    if (features == GLASS_FEATURE_NONE)
+        return source;
+
+    const auto version = source.find("#version");
+    const auto lineEnd = version == std::string::npos ? std::string::npos : source.find('\n', version);
+    if (lineEnd == std::string::npos)
+        return source;
+
+    std::string defines;
+    for (const auto& feature : GLASS_FEATURE_DEFINES)
+        if (features & feature.bit)
+            defines += std::format("#define {} 1\n", feature.name);
+
+    const auto nextLine = 2 + std::count(source.begin(), source.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(lineEnd), '\n');
+    defines += std::format("#line {}\n", nextLine);
+
+    source.insert(lineEnd + 1, defines);
+    return source;
+}

+#ifdef HYPRGLASS_FEATURE_LENS_EDGE
+// ============================================================================
+// LENS EDGE
+// The rim (the edge width) is a convex glass wall edge_height tall: a quarter
+// ellipse across its width, vertical at the edge and flat from its inner side
+// on, so the centre is not bent at all. A vertical eye ray refracts where it
+// enters the rim (Snell's law) and runs down through the glass to the backdrop.
+// ============================================================================
+
+// The edge distance main() uses (bevelSdf), so the rim normal has no crease either.
+float lensEdgeSDF(vec2 uv) {
+    return roundingPower == 2.0 ? getBevelSDF(uv) : getCornerSDF(uv);
+}
+
+// Inward distance, in px, between where the eye ray enters the glass and where
+// it meets the backdrop: n is the surface normal as (inward, up), surfacePx the
+// glass height there. gain scales it, capPx bounds it smoothly.
+float lensBendPx(vec2 n, float surfacePx, float ior, float gain, float capPx) {
+    vec2 ray = refract(vec2(0.0, -1.0), n, 1.0 / ior);
+    float runPx = surfacePx * ray.x / max(-ray.y, 1e-3);
+    return capPx * tanh(clamp(gain * runPx / capPx, -8.0, 8.0));
+}
+
+// At a square or tight corner the rim normal turns all the way round within a
+// few pixels, so every ray there bends toward one point (a dark curl) and the
+// reflection swirls. Fades both out near a corner whose radius is under the rim width.
+float lensCornerFade(vec2 uv, float rimPx) {
+    vec2  halfBox    = glassBoxSizePx * 0.5;
+    vec2  fromCenter = uv * fullSize - glassBoxOffsetPx - halfBox;
+    float radius     = min(pickCornerRadius(fromCenter, radii), min(halfBox.x, halfBox.y));
+    float toCorner   = length(max(halfBox - abs(fromCenter), 0.0));
+    return mix(smoothstep(0.0, rimPx, toCorner), 1.0, clamp(radius / rimPx, 0.0, 1.0));
+}
+#endif

+    // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+    // Contour field (mask_mode = contour, see Contour.hpp). Six passes at a fraction of the
+    // layer's size build a signed distance field of the outline the app draws; they run only
+    // when the layer's content changes. Texel (i, j) is framebuffer row j, like everywhere else.
+    // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+    // Pass 1: which texels are inside the outline. The result is the start of a distance
+    // transform: per channel the squared distance to the nearest texel of the other kind
+    // (r: for an inside texel, to an outside one; g: the opposite), 0 on texels of that
+    // other kind and FAR where nothing is known yet.
+    {"contourseed.frag", R"GLSL(
+#version 300 es
+precision highp float;
+precision highp sampler2D;
+
+uniform sampler2D tex;       // the layer's own render, alpha is the mask
+uniform vec2 boxOffsetPx;    // the layer box in that framebuffer
+uniform vec2 boxSizePx;
+uniform vec2 sourceSizePx;
+uniform float divisor;       // framebuffer px per texel: 1, 2, 4 or 8
+uniform float threshold;     // alpha at which the app's content counts
+
+layout(location = 0) out vec4 fragColor;
+
+const float FAR = 16384.0;   // above any squared distance within reach, and exact in half floats (Contour.hpp)
+
+void main() {
+    vec2 texel = floor(gl_FragCoord.xy);
+
+    // Mean alpha over the texel's block. A tap on the shared corner of 2x2 pixels already
+    // averages them in hardware, so one tap per 2 px, and only the block's own pixels.
+    float taps    = max(divisor * 0.5, 1.0);
+    float stepPx  = divisor > 1.5 ? 2.0 : 1.0;
+    float startPx = divisor > 1.5 ? 1.0 : 0.5;
+    float sum = 0.0;
+    for (float j = 0.0; j < taps; j += 1.0) {
+        for (float i = 0.0; i < taps; i += 1.0) {
+            vec2 p = texel * divisor + vec2(startPx + i * stepPx, startPx + j * stepPx);
+            // never past the box: outside it the layer's render is not cleared, so it holds old frames
+            p = clamp(p, vec2(0.5), boxSizePx - 0.5);
+            // past the monitor the layer is cut off: carry its edge on
+            vec2 q = clamp(boxOffsetPx + p, vec2(0.5), sourceSizePx - 0.5);
+            sum += texture(tex, q / sourceSizePx).a;
+        }
+    }
+
+    bool inside = sum / (taps * taps) >= threshold;
+    fragColor = vec4(inside ? FAR : 0.0, inside ? 0.0 : FAR, 0.0, 1.0);
+}
+)GLSL"},
+
+    // Passes 2 and 3: the exact Euclidean distance transform, one axis at a time (squared
+    // distance = min over the row of (offset^2 + what the previous axis found)), looking at
+    // most reachTexels away. Texels past the box count as outside, so a layer's own edge is an
+    // outline. The last pass turns both distances into one signed distance in framebuffer px,
+    // half a texel in from each class's centre so that zero is the outline itself.
+    {"contouredt.frag", R"GLSL(
+#version 300 es
+precision highp float;
+precision highp int;
+precision highp sampler2D;
+
+uniform sampler2D tex;
+uniform vec2 fieldSize;       // texels
+uniform vec2 direction;       // (1, 0) or (0, 1)
+uniform int reachTexels;
+uniform int resolveSigned;    // 1 on the last pass
+uniform float texelPx;        // framebuffer px per texel
+uniform float reachPx;        // the signed distance is clamped to +-this
+
+layout(location = 0) out vec4 fragColor;
+
+const float FAR = 16384.0;
+
+vec2 known(vec2 p) {
+    if (p.x < 0.0 || p.y < 0.0 || p.x >= fieldSize.x || p.y >= fieldSize.y)
+        return vec2(0.0, FAR);
+    return texelFetch(tex, ivec2(p), 0).rg;
+}
+
+void main() {
+    vec2 p = floor(gl_FragCoord.xy);
+    vec2 best = known(p);
+    for (int k = 1; k <= reachTexels; k++) {
+        float offsetSq = float(k * k);
+        // nothing farther can beat what is found
+        if (offsetSq >= max(best.x, best.y)) break;
+        vec2 step = direction * float(k);
+        best = min(best, min(known(p + step), known(p - step)) + offsetSq);
+    }
+
+    if (resolveSigned == 1) {
+        // an outside texel has best.x == 0: its own squared distance to "outside" is nothing
+        float signedPx = best.x > 0.0 ? -(sqrt(best.x) - 0.5) * texelPx
+                                      :  (sqrt(best.y) - 0.5) * texelPx;
+        fragColor = vec4(clamp(signedPx, -reachPx, reachPx), 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
+    } else {
+        fragColor = vec4(best, 0.0, 1.0);
+    }
+}
+)GLSL"},
+
+    // Passes 4 and 5: a Gaussian along one axis on the signed distance. The distance is linear
+    // through the outline, so this leaves the outline where it is and only rounds the creases
+    // where distances from two edges meet at a corner.
+    {"contoursmooth.frag", R"GLSL(
+#version 300 es
+precision highp float;
+precision highp int;
+precision highp sampler2D;
+
+uniform sampler2D tex;
+uniform vec2 fieldSize;
+uniform vec2 direction;
+uniform int radius;           // taps each side
+uniform float negHalfInvSigmaSq;
+
+layout(location = 0) out vec4 fragColor;
+
+void main() {
+    ivec2 p = ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy);
+    ivec2 last = ivec2(fieldSize) - 1;
+    ivec2 axis = ivec2(direction);
+    float sum = 0.0;
+    float weights = 0.0;
+    for (int k = -radius; k <= radius; k++) {
+        float w = exp(float(k * k) * negHalfInvSigmaSq);
+        sum += w * texelFetch(tex, clamp(p + axis * k, ivec2(0), last), 0).r;
+        weights += w;
+    }
+    fragColor = vec4(sum / weights, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
+}
+)GLSL"},
+
+    // Pass 6: the field the glass shader reads. r: the signed distance, gb: its gradient
+    // (outward, about unit length), so the shader needs one fetch for both.
+    {"contourfield.frag", R"GLSL(
+#version 300 es
+precision highp float;
+precision highp int;
+precision highp sampler2D;
+
+uniform sampler2D tex;
+uniform vec2 fieldSize;
+uniform float texelPx;
+
+layout(location = 0) out vec4 fragColor;
+
+void main() {
+    ivec2 p = ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy);
+    ivec2 last = ivec2(fieldSize) - 1;
+    float centre = texelFetch(tex, p, 0).r;
+    float left   = texelFetch(tex, clamp(p + ivec2(-1, 0), ivec2(0), last), 0).r;
+    float right  = texelFetch(tex, clamp(p + ivec2(1, 0), ivec2(0), last), 0).r;
+    float above  = texelFetch(tex, clamp(p + ivec2(0, -1), ivec2(0), last), 0).r;
+    float below  = texelFetch(tex, clamp(p + ivec2(0, 1), ivec2(0), last), 0).r;
+    fragColor = vec4(centre, vec2(right - left, below - above) / (2.0 * texelPx), 1.0);
+}
 )GLSL"},
 };

+// Mirrors renderLayer()'s abovelock early-out: while locked, every layer is

+// Hyprland draws it in only one of them. Glass queued for the other has no

+static bool layerSkippedForLock(const PHLLS& layerSurface, bool lockscreen) {

+        return true;

+// Frees the contour fields of layers no longer drawn with contour, or every field with

+static void releaseContourFields(bool everything) {

+    }

+// Window glass samples a background Hyprland only composites while the shadow
+// decoration is in the render pipeline: without it, it reads stale or black content.
+static void ensureShadowsForWindowGlass() {
+    if (!g_pGlobalState)
+        return;
+
+    const auto& config = g_pGlobalState->config;
+    // enabled = 0 with hyprglass_enabled tags is a whitelist: it still needs shadows
+    const bool windowGlassInUse = (config.enabled && **config.enabled) ||
+        std::ranges::any_of(g_pGlobalState->decorations, [](const auto& decoration) {
+            auto* glassDecoration = decoration.get();
+            return glassDecoration && glassDecoration->isGlassEnabled();
+        });
+    if (!windowGlassInUse)
+        return;
+
+    const auto shadowEnabled = Config::mgr()->getConfigValue("decoration:shadow:enabled");
+    if (!shadowEnabled.dataptr || !shadowEnabled.type)
+        return;
+
+    if (*shadowEnabled.type != typeid(Config::BOOL))
+        return;
+
+    // Set the value the way hl.config() does at runtime. hyprctl eval would do
+    // the same but also clears the list hyprctl configerrors shows.
+    auto* const shadow = *reinterpret_cast<Config::BOOL* const*>(shadowEnabled.dataptr);
+    if (!*shadow) {
+        *shadow = true;
+        Config::Supplementary::refresher()->scheduleRefresh(Config::Supplementary::REFRESH_WINDOW_STATES);
+    }
+}
+
+// Deferred so the value is never written inside a config reload or a render pass
+void scheduleShadowsForWindowGlass() {
+    if (g_pGlobalState && g_pEventLoopManager)
+        g_pGlobalState->shadowFixLock = g_pEventLoopManager->doLaterLock([] { ensureShadowsForWindowGlass(); });
+}
+
+// ── Hook target resolution ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+// Hyprland 0.57.0 symbols. Itanium mangling encodes the full signature, so a match is exactly the overload the hooks were written for.
+namespace HookSymbols {
+    inline constexpr auto RENDER_LAYER =
+        "_ZN6Render13IHyprRenderer11renderLayerERNS_14CRenderContextEN9Hyprutils6Memory14CSharedPointerIN7Desktop4View13CLayerSurfaceEEENS5_IN7Monitor8CMonitorEEERKNSt6chrono10time_"
+        "pointINSD_3_V212steady_clockENSD_8durationIlSt5ratioILl1ELl1000000000EEEEEEbbb";
+    // The same overload as findFunctionsByName() reports it (nm --demangle=auto): the fallback accepts this exact signature only, so another arity is never hooked.
+    inline constexpr auto RENDER_LAYER_DEMANGLED =
+        "Render::IHyprRenderer::renderLayer(Render::CRenderContext&, Hyprutils::Memory::CSharedPointer<Desktop::View::CLayerSurface>, "
+        "Hyprutils::Memory::CSharedPointer<Monitor::CMonitor>, std::chrono::time_point<std::chrono::_V2::steady_clock, "
+        "std::chrono::duration<long, std::ratio<1l, 1000000000l> > > const&, bool, bool, bool)";
+    inline constexpr auto RENDER_PASS_ADD = "_ZN6Render11CRenderPass3addEON9Hyprutils6Memory14CUniquePointerI12IPassElementEE";
+}
+
+// dlsym first: findFunctionsByName() runs nm on the Hyprland binary, a fork from
+// the compositor that slows down its startup. The dladdr check, against a
+// function the Hyprland executable defines, rejects a same-named symbol from
+// any other loaded object.
+static void* resolveHyprlandFunction(const char* mangledName, const std::string& fallbackSearch, bool (*accept)(const SFunctionMatch&)) {
+    Dl_info hyprland{};
+    Dl_info symbol{};
+    void*   address = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, mangledName);
+    if (address && dladdr(reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HyprlandAPI::findFunctionsByName), &hyprland) && dladdr(address, &symbol) && symbol.dli_fbase == hyprland.dli_fbase)
+        return address;
+
+    for (const auto& match : HyprlandAPI::findFunctionsByName(PHANDLE, fallbackSearch)) {
+        if (accept(match))
+            return match.address;
+    }
+    return nullptr;
+}

     return HYPRLAND_API_VERSION;
 }
 
+// Internal linkage on purpose: never shared with another loaded file.
+static bool s_instanceActive = false;

+// Read by a later copy's LoadGuard::otherActiveCopyPath() through dlsym.
+APICALL EXPORT bool hyprglass_instance_active() {
+    return s_instanceActive;

+// Returning normally keeps Hyprland from reporting a failed load: the copy
+// stays listed, with the reason as its description. addNotification, not V2:
+// V2 passes Hyprland types inside std::any.
+static PLUGIN_DESCRIPTION_INFO pausedDescription(HANDLE handle, const LoadGuard::SVerdict& verdict) {
+    HyprlandAPI::addNotification(handle, std::format("[{}] Paused: {}", PLUGIN_NAME, verdict.message), CHyprColor{1.0, 0.8, 0.2, 1.0}, 10000);
+    return {std::string(PLUGIN_NAME), std::format("Paused ({}): {}", LoadGuard::reasonCode(verdict.reason), verdict.message), std::string(PLUGIN_AUTHOR),
+            std::string(PLUGIN_VERSION)};
+}
